Phase 6D Recommendation Campaign Follow-Up Proposal Administrative Approval
============================================================================

Purpose and recovered roadmap
-----------------------------

Phase 6D records one immutable administrative approval for one exact eligible
Phase 6C follow-up-proposal review event.  The committed Phase 6A roadmap
separates Phase 6B--6F into persistence, review, approval/rejection,
activation, and execution concerns.  Phase 6C uses ``approved`` and
``rejected`` as its review outcomes, but explicitly defines an approved review
as evidence for possible consideration by a later separately authorized
phase.  Phase 6D is that distinct administrative approval boundary; it is not
the later activation or execution boundary.

Only an exact persisted Phase 6C ``approved`` review is eligible.  A rejected,
missing, malformed, stale, identity-inconsistent, or unsupported review cannot
be approved.  Phase 6D never rebuilds the Phase 6A proposal or recomputes the
Phase 5 assessment/policy decision.  It loads the authoritative Phase 6C
record, whose validated repository reload in turn verifies the exact persisted
Phase 6B proposal and complete Phase 6A identity.

Immutable domain contract
-------------------------

``RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval`` is a pure, database-free,
non-assignable contract version 1 value.  It binds:

* the positive generated Phase 6C review-event ID;
* exact Phase 6C review version, canonical text, tagged hash, and approved
  review decision;
* exact reviewed Phase 6B proposal ID and Phase 6A version, canonical text,
  and tagged hash;
* one explicit Phase 6D ``approved`` administrative decision;
* an explicit approver identity; and
* a non-empty administrative reason or basis.

Approver identity is 1--128 ASCII bytes, begins alphanumerically, and permits
only alphanumerics plus ``._@:/+-``.  Reason text is 1--4096 bytes of valid
UTF-8, preserves its exact bytes, requires a byte other than ASCII space, tab,
carriage return, or line feed, and rejects NUL, DEL, and disallowed control
bytes.

Canonical identity
------------------

The authoritative grammar begins with
``experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_v1``.  It
binds the contract version; fixed administrative and negative-authority
semantics; exact review-event, review, and proposal identity; the approved
review and approval decisions; approver; and reason in fixed order.
Delimiter-capable values are byte-length framed.  Integer and length rendering
is locale-independent.  The complete canonical text is limited to 2,113,536
bytes.  Canonical text is authoritative; the tagged FNV-1a hash is only an
accelerator.  Generated approval-event ID, timestamp, PID, scheduler state, and
future lifecycle state remain outside semantic identity.

Persistence, replay, and concurrency
------------------------------------

Migration ``044`` adds the append-only
``experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event``
table.  Restrictive foreign keys bind both the review event and reviewed
proposal.  An invoker-rights insert trigger, with a pinned schema-qualified
execution context, joins the exact Phase 6C review and Phase 6B proposal and
verifies every copied version/canonical/hash field plus approved eligibility.
One review event and one proposal can each have at most one Phase 6D approval.

The repository validates the pure value, takes a review-event-ID-scoped
transaction advisory lock, reloads and validates the complete Phase 6C/6B
chain, and inserts only after exact comparison.  The first valid operation
returns ``recorded``.  An exact retry returns ``existing_identical``.  A
different approver, reason, identity, or payload for the same review returns
``recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_conflict`` and never
replaces history.  Concurrent equivalent requests converge on one event;
concurrent conflicting requests yield one winner and one deterministic
conflict.  Hash equality never substitutes for canonical or typed equality.

Repository lookup by approval-event ID, review-event ID, or proposal ID and
bounded descending list operations are read-only.  They rebuild the immutable
contract, recompute its identity, reload Phase 6C, and validate the complete
upstream binding.  Corrupt or stale persisted evidence fails closed.

Service boundary
----------------

``ApproveRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal`` requires an explicit Phase
6C review-event ID, expected review identity hash, approver, and reason.  In one
transaction it loads the exact review, compares the expected hash, enforces the
approved eligibility decision, builds the pure Phase 6D value, persists it,
and returns the persisted result.  Missing, stale, rejected, malformed, or
conflicting evidence rolls back the transaction.

No main-program CLI or presentation command is added in Phase 6D.  The typed
service is the deliberate application authority for focused verification and
future explicit administrative integration.  This avoids implying that a
newly persisted approval is an activation or execution command.  Read-only
repository inspection remains available without adding operational routing.

Privileges and immutability
---------------------------

Runtime role ``pqxx`` receives only table ``SELECT``, column-scoped payload
``INSERT``, and sequence ``USAGE``.  It cannot provide the generated event ID
or timestamp and has no table-level insert, update, delete, or truncate
privilege.  ``PUBLIC`` receives no table, sequence, or trigger-function
privilege, including PostgreSQL default-ACL fallback.  The provenance trigger
function is not directly executable by runtime or ``PUBLIC``.

Explicit non-authorities
------------------------

Phase 6D administrative approval is immutable audit evidence only.  Its domain
canonical fixes ``activated=false``, ``execution_authorized=false``,
``follow_up_authorized=false``, ``queued=false``, ``scheduled=false``,
``scheduler_started=false``, ``scheduler_signaled=false``,
``workers_started=false``, ``experiments_created=false``,
``experiments_modified=false``, and ``campaign_success_declared=false``.

Phase 6D does not activate or execute a campaign, authorize follow-up
execution, queue or schedule work, signal or start the scheduler, launch a
worker, create or modify an experiment/model/recommendation, advance a
continuation, declare success or profitability, perform statistical
validation, auto-review, or auto-approve.  Negative fields are assertions, not
capabilities.  Later phases continue to own any separately specified
activation and execution authority.

Verification and operational safety
-----------------------------------

Database-free tests cover immutable construction, golden canonical grammar,
deterministic hash, locale independence, exact identity sensitivity,
approved-review eligibility, malformed versions/IDs/hashes, hostile UTF-8 and
control input, operator text, and fixed non-authority.

The repository suite requires an explicitly named clearly disposable database,
rejects case-insensitive ``LSTM``, verifies its connected database before any
migration, and uses per-process schemas.  It covers clean install, upgrade from
pre-existing Phase 6B/6C rows, migration repeatability and ACL fallback,
service eligibility, missing/stale review, exact replay, sequential and
concurrent conflicts, concurrent convergence, rollback, collision/mismatch
failure, append-only privilege denial, restrictive provenance, read-only
lookup/list behavior, and unchanged Phase 6B, Phase 6C, experiment, and
scheduler sentinels.  Test schemas and the disposable database are removed
after verification.

# ADR-0009: Phase 6D separately approves one eligible reviewed follow-up proposal

Status: Accepted
Date: 2026-07-22
Deciders: Project architecture
Affected volumes: Volume VIII §§2–11; Volume XII §§2–11
Supersedes: None
Superseded by: None

## 1. Context and problem statement

Phase 6C records an immutable approved or rejected administrative review for
one exact persisted Phase 6B proposal. Its approved outcome is deliberately
non-authorizing evidence for possible later consideration. The Phase 6A
roadmap also keeps persistence, review, approval/rejection, activation, and
execution as distinct possible Phase 6B–6F concerns. The next increment needs
one explicit approval authority without turning reviewed evidence into
activation, execution, scheduling, or experiment capability.

## 2. Decision

Phase 6D defines a pure immutable administrative approval value, additive
migration 044, an exact-binding append-only repository, and one transactional
service.

- Only an exact persisted Phase 6C `approved` review is eligible. Rejected,
  missing, malformed, stale, or identity-inconsistent reviews fail closed.
- Contract version 1 binds the review-event ID; exact review and proposal
  version/canonical/hash identities; approved review and approval decisions;
  approver; reason; and fixed negative operational semantics.
- Canonical text is authoritative, byte-length framed, locale-independent,
  and bounded. The tagged hash is only an accelerator.
- Migration 044 stores one immutable approval per review/proposal, retains
  restrictive foreign keys, and verifies the full Phase 6C/6B chain in an
  invoker-rights insert trigger with pinned safe `search_path`.
- A review-event-scoped transaction advisory lock serializes approval attempts.
  First persistence is `recorded`, exact replay is `existing_identical`, and
  any changed payload for that review conflicts.
- The service loads and validates the exact Phase 6C record, checks an explicit
  expected review hash and eligibility, builds the pure approval, and persists
  in one transaction.
- Runtime access is SELECT, payload-column INSERT, and sequence USAGE only.
  Existing approvals cannot be updated, deleted, expired, or superseded.
- No CLI, activation adapter, execution adapter, queue, scheduler call,
  worker launch, or experiment/model/recommendation mutation is added.

Administrative approval is evidence only. It is explicitly not follow-up or
execution authorization.

## 3. Rationale and decision drivers

- Preserve the roadmap's separate review and approval authorities despite
  Phase 6C's approved/rejected review terminology.
- Consume authoritative persisted upstream records without recomputing Phase
  5 policy or Phase 6A proposal truth.
- Prevent a stale event ID, hash collision, or copied proposal identity from
  silently approving another review.
- Give retries and concurrent attempts deterministic append-only outcomes.
- Keep every operational capability absent until a later explicit contract.

## 4. Consequences

### 4.1 Positive consequences

- One eligible Phase 6C review can receive at most one immutable Phase 6D
  approval with explicit human identity and basis.
- Full canonical provenance remains inspectable and typed reload detects
  corruption or upstream disagreement.
- Equivalent concurrent requests converge; conflicting requests cannot replace
  the winner.
- Database privileges and schema constraints preserve append-only least
  privilege independently of application validation.

### 4.2 Negative consequences and trade-offs

- Phase 6C already calls its positive review outcome `approved`, so Phase 6D's
  separate approval terminology requires careful documentation.
- Full review and proposal canonical values are duplicated for self-contained
  exact provenance and may produce approval records slightly above two MiB.
- Phase 6D cannot correct a mistaken approval in place; correction would need
  a separately accepted append-only design.
- No main-program CLI is available in this increment; callers use the typed
  service boundary.

### 4.3 Risks and mitigations

- Authority confusion: fixed negative canonical flags, explicit documentation,
  and absence of all action adapters.
- Hash collision: compare complete canonical text and typed payload after every
  hash match; uniqueness is scoped by review/proposal ID, not hash.
- Upstream drift: two restrictive foreign keys, joined provenance trigger, and
  validated Phase 6C repository reload.
- Race or retry ambiguity: transaction advisory lock plus unique review and
  proposal constraints.
- Privilege escalation: explicit PUBLIC/runtime revokes, column grants,
  invoker rights, pinned function context, and NULL-ACL fallback tests.

## 5. Compatibility and migration

Migration 044 is additive after migration 043. It does not alter Phase 6A,
6B, or 6C records, canonical identities, APIs, or privileges and does not touch
experiment or scheduler tables. It supports both an empty clean installation
and upgrade with existing reviewed proposals. Rollback is not performed by the
implementation; any operational rollback would remove only the new Phase 6D
objects under separately approved administration.

## 6. Verification and operational evidence

- Pure warnings-as-errors tests for immutable semantics, canonical grammar,
  deterministic/locale-independent identity, input validation, eligibility,
  and negative authority.
- Isolated PostgreSQL tests for clean and upgrade migration paths,
  repeatability, exact ACL and NULL-ACL fallback, trigger identity/context,
  provenance, service behavior, replay/conflict, concurrency, rollback,
  collision/mismatch rejection, read-only queries, and upstream sentinels.
- Phase 6A/6B/6C focused regressions, project-file validation, focused source
  integration, and whitespace audit. The incremental Release build is deferred
  while active training workers make added build contention unsafe.
- Tests require a clearly disposable non-`LSTM` database and remove all
  disposable database objects after use.

## 7. Alternatives considered

### 7.1 Treat Phase 6C approved review as Phase 6D approval

Rejected because the Phase 6A roadmap separates review from approval and Phase
6C explicitly limits its positive result to possible later consideration.
Collapsing them would erase the next authority boundary rather than implement
it.

### 7.2 Add activation or execution authorization to the approval record

Rejected because administrative approval is evidence, while operational
authorization requires a later explicit lifecycle and safety design.

### 7.3 Rebuild the proposal or policy decision in the service

Rejected because Phase 6B and Phase 6C are authoritative persisted records.
Recomputation could drift and reopen accepted scientific or review decisions.

### 7.4 Add a main-program CLI now

Rejected for this increment because the typed service fully establishes and
tests the authority, while a CLI could imply operational follow-up semantics
before a later activation/execution contract exists.

    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalDecision;

class GroupedNumberPunctuation final : public std::numpunct<char>
{
protected:
    char do_thousands_sep() const override { return ','; }
    std::string do_grouping() const override { return "\1"; }
};

template <typename Function>
void AssertReason(Function&& function, const std::string& expected)
{
    bool threw = false;
    try
    {
        function();
    }
    catch (const std::exception& error)
    {
        threw = true;
        assert(error.what() == expected);
    }
    assert(threw);
}

Review BuildReview(
    ReviewDecision decision = ReviewDecision::approved,
    long long proposalId = 41,
    std::string reviewer = "reviewer@example.test",
    std::string reason = "Review evidence accepted.")
{
    const std::string proposalCanonical = "phase6a-proposal";
    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion,
        proposalId,
        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalContractVersion,
        proposalCanonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(proposalCanonical),
        decision, std::move(reviewer), std::move(reason));
}

Approval Build(
    long long reviewEventId = 73,
    std::string approver = "approver@example.test",
    std::string reason = "Approved after independent review.")
{
    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(reviewEventId,
        BuildReview(), std::move(approver), std::move(reason));
}

} // namespace

int main()
{
    const Review review = BuildReview();
    const Approval approval = Build();
    assert(approval.reviewEventId == 73);
    assert(approval.reviewContractVersion ==
        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion);
    assert(approval.reviewCanonicalText == review.identity.canonicalText);
    assert(approval.reviewIdentityHash == review.identity.hash);
    assert(approval.followUpProposalId == review.followUpProposalId);
    assert(approval.proposalCanonicalText == review.proposalCanonicalText);
    assert(approval.proposalIdentityHash == review.proposalIdentityHash);
    assert(approval.reviewDecision == ReviewDecision::approved);
    assert(approval.decision == ApprovalDecision::approved);
    assert(approval.identity.contractVersion ==
        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalContractVersion);
    assert(approval.identity.hash ==
        RecommendationCanonicalHash(approval.identity.canonicalText));
    ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(approval);
    assert(Build() == approval);

    const std::string expectedCanonical =
        "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_v1"
        ";approval_contract_version=1"
        ";read_only=true"
        ";database_free=true"
        ";persistent=false"
        ";administrative_approval=true"
        ";eligible_review_required=true"
        ";activated=false"
        ";execution_authorized=false"
        ";follow_up_authorized=false"
        ";queued=false"
        ";scheduled=false"
        ";scheduler_started=false"
        ";scheduler_signaled=false"
        ";workers_started=false"
        ";experiments_created=false"
        ";experiments_modified=false"
        ";campaign_success_declared=false"
        ";review_event_id=73"
        ";review_contract_version=1"
        ";review_canonical=" +
        std::to_string(review.identity.canonicalText.size()) + ":" +
        review.identity.canonicalText +
        ";review_identity_hash=24:" + review.identity.hash +
        ";review_decision=approved"
        ";follow_up_proposal_id=41"
        ";proposal_contract_version=1"
        ";proposal_canonical=16:phase6a-proposal"
        ";proposal_identity_hash=24:" + review.proposalIdentityHash +
        ";decision=approved"
        ";approver=21:approver@example.test"
        ";reason=34:Approved after independent review.";
    assert(approval.identity.canonicalText == expectedCanonical);

    const std::locale originalLocale = std::locale();
    std::locale::global(
        std::locale(originalLocale, new GroupedNumberPunctuation));
    const Approval localized = Build();
    std::locale::global(originalLocale);
    assert(localized == approval);

    assert(Build(74).identity != approval.identity);
    assert(Build(73, "other.approver").identity != approval.identity);
    assert(Build(73, "approver@example.test", "Different basis.").identity !=
        approval.identity);
    assert(Build(73,
               std::string(
                   kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproverIdentityBytes,
                   'a'),
               std::string(
                   kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalReasonTextBytes,
                   'x'))
            .reasonText.size() ==
        kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalReasonTextBytes);
    const Review differentReview = BuildReview(
        ReviewDecision::approved, 42);
    assert(BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(73,
               differentReview, "approver@example.test",
               "Approved after independent review.")
            .identity != approval.identity);

    assert(RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalDecisionText(
               ApprovalDecision::approved) == "approved");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalDecisionFromText(
               "approved") == ApprovalDecision::approved);

    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(2, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "approval_contract_unsupported");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(0, review,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "persisted_review_event_id_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]

            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "review_contract_unsupported");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, "fnv1a64:0000000000000000", 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "review_identity_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {

        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            badCanonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(badCanonical), 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");

    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(73,
            BuildReview(ReviewDecision::rejected), "approver", "reason");
    }, "review_not_eligible_for_approval");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            static_cast<ReviewDecision>(99), ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "review_decision_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 0, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "persisted_proposal_id_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 41, 2,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "proposal_contract_unsupported");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, "fnv1a64:0000000000000000",
            ReviewDecision::approved, ApprovalDecision::approved,
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "proposal_identity_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(1, 73, 1,
            review.identity.canonicalText, review.identity.hash, 41, 1,
            review.proposalCanonicalText, review.proposalIdentityHash,
            ReviewDecision::approved, static_cast<ApprovalDecision>(99),
            "approver", "reason");
    }, "approval_decision_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "", "reason");
    }, "approver_identity_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "bad identity", "reason");
    }, "approver_identity_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73,
            std::string(
                kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproverIdentityBytes +
                    1U,
                'a'),
            "reason");
    }, "approver_identity_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "approver", " \t\n\r");
    }, "approval_reason_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "approver", std::string("bad\0reason", 10));
    }, "approval_reason_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "approver", std::string("bad\x01reason", 10));
    }, "approval_reason_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "approver", std::string("\xc3\x28", 2));
    }, "approval_reason_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)Build(73, "approver",
            std::string(
                kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalReasonTextBytes +
                    1U,
                'x'));
    }, "approval_reason_invalid");
    AssertReason([&]
    {
        (void)RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalDecisionFromText(
            "rejected");
    }, "approval_decision_invalid");

    for (const std::string forbidden : {
             "activated=true", "execution_authorized=true",
             "follow_up_authorized=true", "queued=true", "scheduled=true",
             "scheduler_started=true", "scheduler_signaled=true",
             "workers_started=true", "experiments_created=true",
             "experiments_modified=true",
             "campaign_success_declared=true"})
        assert(approval.identity.canonicalText.find(forbidden) ==
            std::string::npos);

    return 0;
}

DO $migration_test$
DECLARE
    approval_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event',
        current_schema());
    review_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event',
        current_schema());
    proposal_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal',
        current_schema());
    approval_sequence text;
    approval_function regprocedure;
BEGIN
    IF to_regclass(approval_table) IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval-event table missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND conname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_review_fk'
          AND contype = 'f'
          AND confrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND confdeltype = 'r') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval review FK missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND conname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_proposal_fk'
          AND contype = 'f'
          AND confrelid = to_regclass(proposal_table)
          AND confdeltype = 'r') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval proposal FK missing';
    END IF;
    IF (
        SELECT count(*) FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND conname IN (
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_review_uidx',
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_proposal_uidx')
          AND contype = 'u') <> 2 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'one-approval-per-review/proposal uniqueness missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes
        WHERE schemaname = current_schema()
          AND tablename =
              'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event'
          AND indexname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_identity_hash_idx')
    THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval hash index missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger
        WHERE tgrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND NOT tgisinternal
          AND tgname =
              'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_provenance_trigger')
    THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval provenance trigger missing';
    END IF;
    IF (
        SELECT count(*) FROM pg_attribute attribute
        JOIN pg_collation coll ON coll.oid = attribute.attcollation
        WHERE attribute.attrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND attribute.attname IN (
              'review_identity_canonical',
              'proposal_identity_canonical',
              'approval_identity_canonical')
          AND coll.collname = 'C') <> 3 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'approval authoritative canonicals are not byte-exact';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
        WHERE table_schema = current_schema()
          AND table_name =
              'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event'
          AND column_name IN (
              'activated', 'execution_authorized', 'follow_up_authorized',
              'queued', 'scheduled', 'scheduler_started',
              'scheduler_signaled', 'workers_started',
              'experiments_created', 'experiments_modified',
              'campaign_success_declared', 'superseded', 'expired')) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'approval-event table contains forbidden authority state';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND contype = 'c'
          AND pg_get_constraintdef(oid) LIKE
              '%review_decision = ''approved''%') OR
       NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND contype = 'c'
          AND pg_get_constraintdef(oid) LIKE
              '%approval_decision = ''approved''%') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'approval eligibility/decision constraint missing';
    END IF;

    IF NOT has_table_privilege('pqxx', approval_table, 'SELECT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval runtime SELECT missing';
    END IF;
    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', approval_table, 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval table INSERT too broad';
    END IF;
    IF NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', approval_table, 'approval_contract_version', 'INSERT') OR
       NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', approval_table, 'review_identity_canonical', 'INSERT') OR
       NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', approval_table, 'approval_identity_canonical', 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval payload INSERT missing';
    END IF;
    IF has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', approval_table,
            'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event_id',
            'INSERT') OR
       has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', approval_table, 'created_at', 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval generated columns writable';
    END IF;
    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', approval_table, 'UPDATE') OR
       has_table_privilege('pqxx', approval_table, 'DELETE') OR
       has_table_privilege('pqxx', approval_table, 'TRUNCATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval mutation privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF has_function_privilege(
           'pqxx',
           'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_provenance()',
           'EXECUTE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger privilege present';
    END IF;

    SELECT trigger.tgfoid::regprocedure
    INTO STRICT approval_function
    FROM pg_trigger trigger
    WHERE trigger.tgrelid = to_regclass(approval_table)
      AND NOT trigger.tgisinternal
      AND trigger.tgname =
          'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_provenance_trigger';
    IF approval_function IS DISTINCT FROM to_regprocedure(format(
            '%I.enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_provenance()',
            current_schema())) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger function incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            function.proacl, acldefault('f', function.proowner))) privilege
        WHERE function.oid = approval_function
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        WHERE function.oid = approval_function
          AND (function.prosecdef OR function.proconfig IS DISTINCT FROM
              ARRAY[format('search_path=pg_catalog, %I, pg_temp',
                  current_schema())])) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger execution context unsafe';
    END IF;

    approval_sequence := pg_get_serial_sequence(
        approval_table,
        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event_id');
    IF NOT has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', approval_sequence, 'USAGE') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', approval_sequence, 'SELECT') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', approval_sequence, 'UPDATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval sequence privilege incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('s', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = approval_sequence::regclass
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval sequence PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('r', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
END
$migration_test$;

struct RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest
{
    long long reviewEventId = 0;
    std::string expectedReviewIdentityHash;
    std::string approverIdentity;
    std::string reasonText;
};

RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest

    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest& request);

    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest& request);

bool IsValidCanonicalHash(const std::string& value)
{
    constexpr std::string_view prefix = "fnv1a64:";
    return value.size() == prefix.size() + 16U &&
        value.compare(0, prefix.size(), prefix) == 0 &&
        std::all_of(value.begin() +
                static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(prefix.size()),
            value.end(),
            [](unsigned char character)
            {
                return (character >= '0' && character <= '9') ||
                    (character >= 'a' && character <= 'f');
            });
}

} // namespace

RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest
ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest(
    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest& request)
{
    if (request.reviewEventId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_review_event_id_invalid");
    if (!IsValidCanonicalHash(request.expectedReviewIdentityHash))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_expected_review_hash_invalid");
    return request;
}

RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalPersistResult
ApproveRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal(
    pqxx::connection& connection,
    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest& request)
{
    const auto validated =
        ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalRequest(request);
    pqxx::work transaction{connection};
    if (!RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewSchemaExists(transaction) ||
        !RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalSchemaExists(transaction))
        throw std::runtime_error(
            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_schemas_required");
    const auto persistedReview =
        FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
            transaction, validated.reviewEventId);
    if (!persistedReview)
        throw std::runtime_error(
            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_review_not_found");
    if (persistedReview->review.identity.hash !=
        validated.expectedReviewIdentityHash)
        throw std::runtime_error(
            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_review_identity_mismatch");
    if (persistedReview->review.decision !=
        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision::approved)
        throw std::runtime_error(
            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_review_not_eligible");
    const auto approval =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(
            persistedReview->reviewEventId, persistedReview->review,
            validated.approverIdentity, validated.reasonText);
    auto result = PersistRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(
        transaction, approval);
    transaction.commit();
    return result;
}

} // namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation

std::string EnvironmentOr(const char* name, const char* fallback)
{
    const char* value = std::getenv(name);
    return value && *value ? value : fallback;
}

std::string ReadFile(const std::string& path)
{
    std::ifstream input{path};
    if (!input) throw std::runtime_error("unable_to_read_" + path);
    return {std::istreambuf_iterator<char>{input},
        std::istreambuf_iterator<char>{}};
}

std::string Lowercase(std::string value)
{
    std::transform(value.begin(), value.end(), value.begin(),
        [](unsigned char character)
        { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(character)); });
    return value;
}

bool SafeDisposableDatabaseName(const std::string& value)
{
    if (value.empty()) return false;
    const std::string lower = Lowercase(value);
    if (lower == "lstm" ||
        (lower.find("test") == std::string::npos &&
            lower.find("tmp") == std::string::npos &&
            lower.find("disposable") == std::string::npos))
        return false;
    return std::all_of(value.begin(), value.end(),
        [](unsigned char character)
        {
            return std::isalnum(character) || character == '_' ||
                character == '-';
        });
}

bool PermissionDenied(const pqxx::sql_error& error)
{
    return error.sqlstate() == "42501" ||
        std::string(error.what()).find("permission denied") !=
            std::string::npos;
}

void SetSearchPath(
    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
    const std::string& schema)
{
    transaction.exec(
        "SET LOCAL search_path TO " + transaction.quote_name(schema) + ";");
}

void CreateBaseSchema(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema)
{
    pqxx::work setup{owner};
    setup.exec("CREATE SCHEMA " + setup.quote_name(schema) + ";");
    SetSearchPath(setup, schema);
    setup.exec(R"SQL(
CREATE TABLE experiment_recommendation_campaign_approval(
    recommendation_campaign_approval_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    approval_identity_canonical text NOT NULL,
    approval_identity_hash text NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization(
    recommendation_campaign_materialization_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    recommendation_campaign_approval_id bigint NOT NULL,
    materialization_contract_version integer NOT NULL,
    approval_identity_hash text NOT NULL,
    selected_member_count integer NOT NULL,
    materialization_identity_canonical text NOT NULL,
    materialization_identity_hash text NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization_member(
    recommendation_campaign_materialization_member_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    recommendation_campaign_materialization_id bigint NOT NULL,
    member_ordinal integer NOT NULL,
    recommendation_ranking_member_id bigint NOT NULL,
    recommendation_id bigint NOT NULL,
    source_experiment_id bigint NOT NULL,
    recommendation_conversion_proposal_id bigint NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE experiment(placeholder text PRIMARY KEY);
CREATE TABLE experiment_scheduler(placeholder text PRIMARY KEY);
INSERT INTO experiment VALUES('unchanged');
INSERT INTO experiment_scheduler VALUES('unchanged');
)SQL");
    setup.exec("GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA " + setup.quote_name(schema) +
        " TO pqxx; GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA " +
        setup.quote_name(schema) + " TO pqxx;");
    setup.commit();
}

void ApplyMigration(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema,
    const std::string& path)
{
    pqxx::work migration{owner};
    SetSearchPath(migration, schema);
    migration.exec(ReadFile(path));
    migration.commit();
}

void DropSchema(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema)
{
    pqxx::work cleanup{owner};
    cleanup.exec("DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS " + cleanup.quote_name(schema) +
        " CASCADE;");
    cleanup.commit();
}

Context ComparisonContext(int seed)
{
    return {"eurusd", 12, 0.01, 24,
        "phase6d-label-" + std::to_string(seed), "2026-06-01",
        "2026-06-30"};
}

Metric Numeric(std::string identity, double value)
{
    return {std::move(identity), value, MetricSupport::NumericDelta};
}

MemberEvidence FavorableMember(int seed)
{
    MemberIdentity identity(1, 1000 + seed, 2000 + seed, 3000 + seed,
        4000 + seed, 5000 + seed, 6000 + seed);
    return {identity, Lifecycle::Succeeded,
        SourceEvidence(identity.sourceExperimentId, 7000 + seed,
            8000 + seed, ComparisonContext(seed),
            MetricCollection({Numeric("inference_accuracy", 0.70),
                Numeric("leader_score", 0.80)})),
        ResultEvidence(identity.expectedExperimentId, 9000 + seed,
            {ResultIdentity{"inference", 10000 + seed}},
            ComparisonContext(seed),
            MetricCollection({Numeric("inference_accuracy", 0.75),
                Numeric("leader_score", 0.85)}))};
}

Proposal BuildProposal(int seed)
{
    const std::string campaignCanonical =
        "phase6d-campaign-" + std::to_string(seed);
    CampaignIdentity campaign(100 + seed, campaignCanonical,
        RecommendationCanonicalHash(campaignCanonical));
    const std::string materializationCanonical =
        "phase6d-materialization-" + std::to_string(seed);
    MaterializationIdentity materialization(200 + seed,
        campaign.campaignApprovalId, campaign.identityHash, 1, 1,
        materializationCanonical,
        RecommendationCanonicalHash(materializationCanonical));
    const Assessment assessment =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(campaign,
            materialization, "2026-07-22 12:00:00+00",
            {FavorableMember(seed)});
    const auto policy = BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(PolicyInput{});
    const auto policyDecision = ApplyRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
        policy, assessment);
    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal(
        assessment, policyDecision);
}

PersistedRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview PersistReview(
    const std::string& runtimeConnectionString,
    long long proposalId,
    const Proposal& proposal,
    ReviewDecision decision,
    const std::string& reviewer,
    const std::string& reason)
{
    const Review review = BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
        proposalId, proposal, decision, reviewer, reason);
    pqxx::connection runtime{runtimeConnectionString};
    pqxx::work transaction{runtime};
    const auto result =
        PersistRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
            transaction, review);
    assert(result.outcome ==
        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPersistOutcome::recorded);
    transaction.commit();
    return result.persisted;
}

std::string TableSnapshot(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema,
    const std::string& table,
    const std::string& orderColumn)
{
    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
    SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
    return transaction.exec(
        "SELECT coalesce(string_agg(row_to_json(snapshot)::text,'|' ORDER BY " +
        orderColumn + "),'') FROM (SELECT * FROM " +
        transaction.quote_name(table) + ") snapshot;")
        .one_row()[0].as<std::string>();
}

long long TableCount(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema,
    const std::string& table)
{
    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
    SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
    return transaction.exec(
        "SELECT count(*) FROM " + transaction.quote_name(table) + ";")
        .one_row()[0].as<long long>();
}

std::pair<long long, bool> ApprovalSequenceState(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema)
{
    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
    SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
    const std::string sequence = transaction.exec(
        "SELECT relation.relname FROM pg_class relation WHERE relation.oid="
        "pg_get_serial_sequence('experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_"
        "up_proposal_approval_event','recommendation_campaign_follow_up_"
        "proposal_approval_event_id')::regclass;")
                                     .one_row()[0].as<std::string>();
    const auto row = transaction.exec(
        "SELECT last_value,is_called FROM " +
        transaction.quote_name(sequence) + ";")
                         .one_row();
    return {row[0].as<long long>(), row[1].as<bool>()};
}

template <typename Function>
void AssertRuntimeError(Function&& function, const std::string& expected)
{
    bool rejected = false;
    try
    {
        function();
    }
    catch (const std::runtime_error& error)
    {
        rejected = error.what() == expected;
    }
    assert(rejected);
}

template <typename Function>
void AssertInvalidArgument(Function&& function, const std::string& expected)
{
    bool rejected = false;
    try
    {
        function();
    }
    catch (const std::invalid_argument& error)
    {
        rejected = error.what() == expected;
    }
    assert(rejected);
}

template <typename Corruption>
void AssertMalformedReloadRejected(
    pqxx::connection& owner,
    const std::string& schema,
    long long approvalEventId,
    Corruption&& corruption,
    const std::string& expectedFragment)
{
    bool rejected = false;
    {
        pqxx::work transaction{owner};
        SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
        corruption(transaction);
        try
        {
            (void)FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(
                transaction, approvalEventId);
        }
        catch (const std::runtime_error& error)
        {
            const std::string message = error.what();
            rejected = message.starts_with(
                           "invalid_persisted_recommendation_campaign_"
                           "follow_up_proposal_approval:") &&
                message.find(expectedFragment) != std::string::npos;
        }
    }
    assert(rejected);
}

int main()
{
    const char* testDatabase = std::getenv("LSTM_TEST_DB_NAME");
    if (testDatabase == nullptr || *testDatabase == '\0')
    {
        std::cerr << "LSTM_TEST_DB_NAME_required\n";
        return 2;
    }
    const std::string database = testDatabase;
    if (!SafeDisposableDatabaseName(database))
    {
        std::cerr << "clearly_disposable_non_LSTM_database_required\n";
        return 2;
    }
    const std::string host = EnvironmentOr("LSTM_TEST_DB_HOST", "127.0.0.1");
    const std::string port = EnvironmentOr("LSTM_TEST_DB_PORT", "5432");
    const std::string ownerUser = EnvironmentOr(
        "LSTM_TEST_DB_ADMIN_USER", EnvironmentOr("USER", "vjp").c_str());
    const std::string suffix = std::to_string(getpid());
    const std::string cleanSchema = "phase6d_approval_clean_" + suffix;
    const std::string upgradeSchema = "phase6d_approval_upgrade_" + suffix;
    const std::string ownerConnectionString =
        "host=" + host + " port=" + port + " user=" + ownerUser +
        " dbname=" + database;
    const auto runtimeConnectionString = [&](const std::string& schema)
    {
        return "host=" + host + " port=" + port +
            " user=pqxx dbname=" + database +
            " options='-c search_path=" + schema + "'";

                                         bool& recorded,

                                         std::exception_ptr& error)
        {

            {
                pqxx::connection connection{runtime};
                while (!startConflict.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
                    std::this_thread::yield();
                const auto result =
                    ApproveRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal(
                        connection, request);
                recorded = result.outcome == ApprovalOutcome::recorded;

                rejected = std::string(exception.what()) ==
                    "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_conflict";
                if (!rejected) error = std::current_exception();
            }

                error = std::current_exception();

            std::ref(conflictError1));
        std::thread conflict2(approveConflict, std::cref(request3b),
            std::ref(conflictRecorded2), std::ref(conflictRejected2),
            std::ref(conflictError2));
        startConflict.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
        conflict1.join();
        conflict2.join();
        if (conflictError1) std::rethrow_exception(conflictError1);
        if (conflictError2) std::rethrow_exception(conflictError2);
        assert(conflictRecorded1 != conflictRecorded2);
        assert(conflictRejected1 != conflictRejected2);
        assert(conflictRecorded1 == conflictRejected2);
        assert(conflictRecorded2 == conflictRejected1);

        }

        try
        {

            ApprovalSequenceState(owner, upgradeSchema);
        {
            pqxx::connection connection{runtime};

                "SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ, READ ONLY;");
            const auto found =

                transaction, 999999));
            assert(!FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalByReviewEventId(
                transaction, 999999));
            assert(!FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalByProposalId(
                transaction, 999999));
            const auto listed =
                ListRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovals(
                    transaction, 2);
            assert(listed.size() == 2);
            assert(listed[0].approvalEventId > listed[1].approvalEventId);

                       "SELECT count(*) FROM pg_locks WHERE "

                       .one_row()[0].as<int>() == 0);
        }

            sequenceBeforeReads);
        AssertInvalidArgument([&]
        {
            pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
            SetSearchPath(transaction, upgradeSchema);
            (void)ListRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovals(
                transaction, 0);
        }, "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_list_limit_invalid");
        AssertInvalidArgument([&]
        {
            pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
            SetSearchPath(transaction, upgradeSchema);

                transaction,
                kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalListLimit +
                    1);
        }, "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_list_limit_invalid");

        assert(TableSnapshot(owner, upgradeSchema,
                   "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal",
                   "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id") ==
            proposalSnapshotBefore);
        assert(TableSnapshot(owner, upgradeSchema,

            reviewSnapshotBefore);
        assert(TableSnapshot(owner, upgradeSchema, "experiment", "placeholder") ==
            experimentSnapshotBefore);
        assert(TableSnapshot(
                   owner, upgradeSchema, "experiment_scheduler", "placeholder") ==
            schedulerSnapshotBefore);

        DropSchema(owner, upgradeSchema);
    }
    catch (...)
    {
        try
        {
            DropSchema(owner, cleanSchema);
        }
        catch (...)
        {
        }
        try
        {
            DropSchema(owner, upgradeSchema);
        }
        catch (...)
        {
        }
        throw;
    }

    return 0;

-- Phase 6C: append-only administrative operator-review decisions for exact
-- persisted Phase 6B proposals. Approval here grants no activation,
-- execution, follow-up, queue, scheduler, worker, or experiment authority.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event (
    recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id bigserial
        PRIMARY KEY CHECK (
            recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id > 0),
    review_contract_version integer NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_contract_version = 1),
    recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id bigint NOT NULL,
    proposal_contract_version integer NOT NULL CHECK (
        proposal_contract_version = 1),
    proposal_identity_canonical text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        proposal_identity_canonical <> '' AND
        octet_length(proposal_identity_canonical) <= 1048576),
    proposal_identity_hash text NOT NULL CHECK (
        proposal_identity_hash ~ '^fnv1a64:[0-9a-f]{16}$'),
    review_decision text NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_decision IN ('approved', 'rejected')),
    reviewer_identity text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        reviewer_identity ~
            '^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._@:/+\-]{0,127}$'),
    reason_text text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        reason_text <> '' AND octet_length(reason_text) <= 4096),
    review_identity_canonical text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_identity_canonical <> '' AND
        octet_length(review_identity_canonical) <= 1056768),
    review_identity_hash text NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_identity_hash ~ '^fnv1a64:[0-9a-f]{16}$'),
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_proposal_fk
        FOREIGN KEY (recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id)
        REFERENCES experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal(
            recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_once_uidx
        UNIQUE (recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_identity_hash_idx
    ON experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event(
        review_identity_hash);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION
enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY INVOKER
AS $$
BEGIN
    PERFORM 1
    FROM experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal proposal
    WHERE proposal.recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id =
              NEW.recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id
      AND proposal.proposal_contract_version =
              NEW.proposal_contract_version
      AND proposal.proposal_identity_canonical =
              NEW.proposal_identity_canonical
      AND proposal.proposal_identity_hash = NEW.proposal_identity_hash;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review provenance mismatch'
            USING ERRCODE = '23514',
                  CONSTRAINT =
                      'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_check';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DO $$
DECLARE review_schema text := current_schema();
BEGIN
    EXECUTE format(
        'ALTER FUNCTION %I.enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance() '
        'SET search_path TO pg_catalog, %I, pg_temp',
        review_schema, review_schema);
END $$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS
enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger
ON experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event;
CREATE TRIGGER
enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger
BEFORE INSERT ON
    experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION
    enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance();

COMMENT ON TABLE
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event IS
    'Immutable Phase 6C administrative review only; never activation, execution, scheduling, experiment mutation, or follow-up authorization.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event.review_identity_canonical IS
    'Authoritative byte-exact Phase 6C review identity; the tagged hash is only an accelerator.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event.review_decision IS
    'Administrative approved or rejected outcome only; approval grants no action authority.';

REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON
    experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
    FROM PUBLIC, pqxx;
REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON FUNCTION
    enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()
    FROM PUBLIC, pqxx;

GRANT SELECT ON
    experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
    TO pqxx;
GRANT INSERT (
    review_contract_version,
    recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id,
    proposal_contract_version,
    proposal_identity_canonical,
    proposal_identity_hash,
    review_decision,
    reviewer_identity,
    reason_text,
    review_identity_canonical,
    review_identity_hash)
ON experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
TO pqxx;

DO $$
DECLARE sequence_name text;
BEGIN
    sequence_name := pg_get_serial_sequence(
        'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event',
        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id');
    EXECUTE format(
        'REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCE %s FROM PUBLIC', sequence_name);
    EXECUTE format(
        'REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCE %s FROM pqxx', sequence_name);
    EXECUTE format(
        'GRANT USAGE ON SEQUENCE %s TO pqxx', sequence_name);
END $$;
DO $migration_test$
DECLARE
    review_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event',
        current_schema());
    proposal_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal',
        current_schema());
    review_sequence text;
    review_function regprocedure;
BEGIN
    IF to_regclass(review_table) IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review-event table missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND conname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_proposal_fk'
          AND contype = 'f'
          AND confrelid = to_regclass(proposal_table)
          AND confdeltype = 'r') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review restrictive FK missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND conname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_once_uidx'
          AND contype = 'u') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'one-review-per-proposal uniqueness missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes
        WHERE schemaname = current_schema()
          AND tablename =
              'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event'
          AND indexname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_identity_hash_idx')
    THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review hash index missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger
        WHERE tgrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND NOT tgisinternal
          AND tgname =
              'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger')
    THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review provenance trigger missing';
    END IF;
    IF (
        SELECT count(*) FROM pg_attribute attribute
        JOIN pg_collation coll ON coll.oid = attribute.attcollation
        WHERE attribute.attrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND attribute.attname IN (
              'proposal_identity_canonical',
              'review_identity_canonical')
          AND coll.collname = 'C') <> 2 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'review authoritative canonicals are not byte-exact';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
        WHERE table_schema = current_schema()
          AND table_name =
              'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event'
          AND column_name IN (
              'activated', 'execution_authorized', 'follow_up_authorized',
              'queued', 'scheduled', 'scheduler_started',
              'scheduler_signaled', 'workers_started',
              'experiments_created', 'experiments_modified',
              'campaign_success_declared', 'superseded', 'expired')) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'review-event table contains forbidden authority state';
    END IF;

    IF NOT has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'SELECT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review runtime SELECT missing';
    END IF;
    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review table INSERT too broad';
    END IF;
    IF NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_contract_version', 'INSERT') OR
       NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_decision', 'INSERT') OR
       NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_identity_canonical', 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review payload INSERT missing';
    END IF;
    IF has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table,
            'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id',
            'INSERT') OR
       has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'created_at', 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review generated columns writable';
    END IF;
    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'UPDATE') OR
       has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'DELETE') OR
       has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'TRUNCATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review mutation privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF has_function_privilege(
           'pqxx',
           'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()',
           'EXECUTE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger privilege present';
    END IF;

    SELECT trigger.tgfoid::regprocedure
    INTO STRICT review_function
    FROM pg_trigger trigger
    WHERE trigger.tgrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
      AND NOT trigger.tgisinternal
      AND trigger.tgname =
          'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger';
    IF review_function IS DISTINCT FROM to_regprocedure(format(
            '%I.enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()',
            current_schema())) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger function incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            function.proacl, acldefault('f', function.proowner))) privilege
        WHERE function.oid = review_function
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        WHERE function.oid = review_function
          AND (function.prosecdef OR function.proconfig IS DISTINCT FROM
              ARRAY[format('search_path=pg_catalog, %I, pg_temp',
                  current_schema())])) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger execution context unsafe';
    END IF;

    review_sequence := pg_get_serial_sequence(
        review_table,
        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id');
    IF NOT has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'USAGE') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'SELECT') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'UPDATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review sequence privilege incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('s', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = review_sequence::regclass
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review sequence PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('r', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
END
$migration_test$;

ExpertAdvisor.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj: OK

codex
Two substantive issues are emerging from inspection:

- The committed roadmap distinguishes “review” from “approval/rejection,” but Phase 6C already records the approve/reject decision, reviewer, and reason. Phase 6D currently adds another approve-only decision, approver, and reason without defining a different question, role, policy, threshold, or effect. That is presently a blocking authority-boundary ambiguity, not merely a naming blemish.
- The documentation claims explicit NULL-ACL fallback coverage, but the Phase 6D suite only applies `coalesce(..., acldefault(...))` to objects whose ACLs were made non-NULL by migration grants/revokes; it does not force or independently exercise the NULL branch.

I’ll continue the safe verification so the final verdict separates architectural blocking from mechanical correctness.

codex
The focused repository compile reached only a third-party-header issue: libpqxx 7.10 uses C++23 `[[assume]]` in headers while this project is C++20, so treating Homebrew headers as ordinary `-I` made their extension warning fatal. The project source was not implicated. I’m rerunning with libpqxx correctly marked as a system include, preserving warnings-as-errors for repository code.

DECLARE
    table_oid oid := '"'null_acl_table'::regclass;
    sequence_oid oid := 'null_acl_sequence'::regclass;
    function_oid oid := 'null_acl_function()'::regprocedure;
BEGIN

END

Approval Build(
    long long reviewEventId = 73,
    std::string approver = "approver@example.test",
    std::string reason = "Approved after independent review.")
{
    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(reviewEventId,
        BuildReview(), std::move(approver), std::move(reason));
}

} // namespace

int main()
{
    const Review review = BuildReview();
    const Approval approval = Build();
    assert(approval.reviewEventId == 73);
    assert(approval.reviewContractVersion ==
        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion);
    assert(approval.reviewCanonicalText == review.identity.canonicalText);
    assert(approval.reviewIdentityHash == review.identity.hash);
    assert(approval.followUpProposalId == review.followUpProposalId);
    assert(approval.proposalCanonicalText == review.proposalCanonicalText);
    assert(approval.proposalIdentityHash == review.proposalIdentityHash);
    assert(approval.reviewDecision == ReviewDecision::approved);
    assert(approval.decision == ApprovalDecision::approved);
    assert(approval.identity.contractVersion ==
        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApprovalContractVersion);
    assert(approval.identity.hash ==
        RecommendationCanonicalHash(approval.identity.canonicalText));
    ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalApproval(approval);
    assert(Build() == approval);

    const std::string expectedCanonical =
        "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_v1"
        ";approval_contract_version=1"
        ";read_only=true"
        ";database_free=true"
        ";persistent=false"
        ";administrative_approval=true"
        ";eligible_review_required=true"
        ";activated=false"
        ";execution_authorized=false"
        ";follow_up_authorized=false"
        ";queued=false"
        ";scheduled=false"
        ";scheduler_started=false"
        ";scheduler_signaled=false"
        ";workers_started=false"
        ";experiments_created=false"
          'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_provenance_trigger';
    IF approval_function IS DISTINCT FROM to_regprocedure(format(
            '%I.enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_provenance()',
            current_schema())) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger function incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            function.proacl, acldefault('f', function.proowner))) privilege
        WHERE function.oid = approval_function
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        WHERE function.oid = approval_function
          AND (function.prosecdef OR function.proconfig IS DISTINCT FROM
              ARRAY[format('search_path=pg_catalog, %I, pg_temp',
                  current_schema())])) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval trigger execution context unsafe';
    END IF;

    approval_sequence := pg_get_serial_sequence(
        approval_table,
        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_approval_event_id');
    IF NOT has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', approval_sequence, 'USAGE') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', approval_sequence, 'SELECT') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', approval_sequence, 'UPDATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval sequence privilege incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('s', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = approval_sequence::regclass
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval sequence PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('r', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = to_regclass(approval_table)
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal approval PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
END
$migration_test$;
